Which means no more crying over spilled milk 😉. Still available in your favourite colours, with a premium matte-finish.</p>
<p>It is number #1 sippy cup in Singapore!</p>
<p>As Little Baby is the exclusive distributor for B.Box, you may also find the products from our authorised retail partners in Singapore and Malaysia via www.bbox.com.sg</p></div>

    window.sswAddEventListener('sswjqueryload', function () {
      var path_name = window.location.pathname;
      var ssw_is_mobile = +/Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
      if (path_name.indexOf('pages/feed') == -1 && path_name.indexOf('pages/profile') == -1 && path_name.indexOf('pages/profile-edit') == -1) {
        var data = {'_sid': sswCookie('hesid'), 'hash_key': sswCookie('hash_key'), 'module': 'feed', 'name': 'community', 'is_mobile': ssw_is_mobile };
        
        ssw.ajax({
          url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/core/widget/index',
          data: data,
          dataType: 'html',
          success: function (response) {
            if (ssw('#site-body').length) {
              ssw('#site-body').append(response);
            } else {
              ssw('body').append(response);
            }
          }
        });
      }
    });

          new_objects = new_objects.find(selector);
          if (new_objects.length) {
            var c = 0, column_count = feed_box_width / 220;
            ssw.each(new_objects , function(k, item){
              new_elements.push(ssw(item));
              if (ssw('.ssw-feed-item').length < 3) {
                ssw('#' + column_name + '-' + (c % column_count + 1)).append(ssw(item));
              } else {
                ssw('#' + column_name + '-' + (c % column_count + 1)).prepend(ssw(item));
              }
              ssw(item).fadeIn((c + 1) * 1000);
              if (c ++ == 0) {
                self.setLastId(parseInt(ssw(item).attr('id').split('-').pop()));
              }
              self.setStartId(parseInt(ssw(item).attr('id').split('-').pop()));
            });
            if (elements.length == 0) {
              self.addNewElementsAsFisrt(new_elements);
              self.resize();
            } else {
              self.addNewElementsAsFisrt(new_elements);
              self.resize();
            }
          }
        },
        dataType: 'html'
      }).done(function(){
      });
      ssw('#ssw-feed-updates-count').hide();
    };
    this.newItemChecker = function(){
      if (typeof self.getLastId() === 'undefined' || self.getLastId() == null) {
        return;
      }
      data = {
        module: 'feed',
        name: 'feed',
        'type': self.getType(),
        'count': true,
        'start-id': self.getLastId(),
        direction: 'UP',
        _sid: ssw.cookie('hesid'),
        hash_key : ssw.cookie('hash_key')
      };
      ssw.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/core/widget/index',
        data: data,
        success: function (response_data) {
          var json =  response_data;
          if(json.count > 0) {
            if (json.count == 1) {
              ssw('#ssw-feed-updates-count > #new-pins-count').html(sswLangs.t("quantity_new_update", [json.count]));
            } else {
              ssw('#ssw-feed-updates-count > #new-pins-count').html(sswLangs.t("quantity_new_updates", [json.count]));
            }
            ssw('#ssw-feed-updates-count').show();
          }
        },
        dataType: 'json'
      });
    };
    this.loadMore = function(callback) {
      scrollChecker = 1;
      data = {
        _sid: ssw.cookie('hesid'),
        module: 'feed',
        name: 'feed',
        type: self.getType(),
        'is-partially': true,
        'start-id': self.getStartId(),
        direction: 'DOWN',
        hash_key : ssw.cookie('hash_key')
      };
      if (!(self.getType() == 'recommended' || self.getType() == 'all')) {
        data.name = data.type;
        data.user_id = ssw('#com-user-id').val();
      }

      ssw.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/core/widget/index',
        beforeSend: function(){
          ssw('span#ajax-loader').show();
          ssw('.ssw-feed-load-more').hide();
        },
        data: data,
        success: (typeof onSuccess !== 'undefined')? onSuccess : (function (response_data) {
        if('function' == typeof callback )
          callback(response_data);
          var new_elements = ssw('<div>').html(response_data), i, min;
          new_elements = new_elements.find(selector);
          var column_count = feed_box_width / 220;
          if (new_elements.length > 0) {
//            var c = 0;
            ssw.each(new_elements, function(k, item){
              self.addNewElement(ssw(item));
              min = ssw('#' + column_name + '-1');
              for (i = 2; i <= column_count; i ++) {
                if (min.actual('outerHeight') > ssw('#' + column_name + '-' + i).actual('outerHeight')) {
                  min = ssw('#' + column_name + '-' + i);
                }
              }
//              if (c ++ == 0) {
//                self.setLastId(parseInt(ssw(item).attr('id').split('-').pop()));
//              }
              self.setStartId(parseInt(ssw(item).attr('id').split('-').pop()));
              ssw(item).show();
              min.append(ssw(item));
            });
            sswLazyLoad(document.querySelector('#ssw-community-box'), '.ssw-item-image img');
            scrollChecker = 0;
          } else {
              setTimeout(function () {
                ssw('.ssw-feed-load-more').hide();
              }, 0);
            ssw('span.end-reached').show();
          }
        }),
        dataType: 'html'
      }).done(function(){
        ssw('.ssw-feed-load-more').removeAttr('style');
        ssw('span#ajax-loader').hide();
      });
    };
        this.onScroll = this.loadMore;
        
    this.getWidth = function() {
      return feed_box_width;
    };

    this.resize = function(){
      if (elements.length == 0) {
        return;
      }
      feed_box_width = container.actual('width');
      var column_count = parseInt(feed_box_width / pinWidth), i, min;
      var dim = ((feed_box_width % pinWidth) - (16 * column_count - 2) + 10) / column_count;
      container.empty();
      for (var i = 0; i < column_count; i ++) {
        container.append(ssw('<div>', {class:'ssw-feed-column', id: column_name + '-' + (i + 1)}));
      }
      var c = 0;

      ssw.each(elements, function(k, item){
        min = ssw('#' + column_name + '-1');
        for (i = 2; i <= column_count; i ++) {
          if (min.actual('outerHeight') > ssw('#' + column_name + '-' + i).actual('outerHeight')) {
            min = ssw('#' + column_name + '-' + i);
          }
        }
        ssw(item).show();
        min.append(ssw(item));
        var obj;
          try {
              eval('obj = '+ssw('textarea[name=obj]', item).val());
          } catch(e) {}
        var imageHeight = 0;
        if(obj && obj.image_dim.length !== 0){
          obj.image_dim = obj.image_dim == '0x0' ? '1x1' : obj.image_dim;
          imageHeight = parseInt(obj.image_dim.split('x')[1]);
          var imageWidth = parseInt(obj.image_dim.split('x')[0]),
            percent = 0;

          if( ssw('.ssw-item-image, .ssw-item-video', ssw(item)).length > 0 ){
            if(imageWidth > pinWidth){
              percent = 100 - (pinWidth/imageWidth)*100;
              imageHeight = imageHeight - (percent) * (imageHeight/100);
            } else {
              percent = (pinWidth/imageWidth)*100 - 100 ;
              imageHeight = imageHeight + (percent) * (imageHeight/100);
            }

            ssw('.ssw-item-image, .ssw-item-video', ssw(item)).css('height', imageHeight);
          }
        }
        if (c ++ == 0) {
          self.setLastId(parseInt(ssw(item).attr('id').split('-').pop()));
        }
        self.setStartId(parseInt(ssw(item).attr('id').split('-').pop()));
      });
      ssw('#' + container.attr('id') + ' .ssw-feed-column').css('width', pinWidth + dim);
    };

    this.reloader = function() {
      container.css('opacity', 0.1);
      var data = {
        module: 'feed',
        feed_type: 'community',
        name: 'feed',
        type: type,
        '_sid': ssw.cookie('hesid'),
        'hash_key': ssw.cookie('hash_key')
      };
      ssw.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/core/widget/index',
        data: data,
        success: function (response_data) {
          container.empty();
          var new_objects = ssw('<div>').html(response_data);
          new_objects = new_objects.find('.ssw-feed-item');
          if (new_objects.length > 0) {
            self.updateElements(new_objects);
            self.resize();
            scrollChecker = 0;
            sswLazyLoad(document.querySelector('#ssw-community-box'), '.ssw-item-image img');
            ssw('span.end-reached').hide();
          } else {
            ssw('#ssw-feed-content').html('<div id="feed-is-empty" style="text-align: center; display: block;">' + sswLangs.t('feed_is_empty') + '</div>');
          }
          container.css('opacity', 1);
          ssw('#feed-switch-loader').hide();
        },
        dataType: 'html'
      });
    };
  };
  ssw(document).ready(function(){
    sswLibraryLoaded(function () {
      pinView = new PinView(ssw('#ssw-feed-content'), ssw('#ssw-feed-content .ssw-feed-item'), '.ssw-feed-item');
      pinView.resize();
      var redraw_cb = function() {
        if(ssw(window).width() != sswCmWindowWidth){
          sswCmWindowWidth = ssw(window).width();
          if (ssw('#ssw-feed-content:visible').length > 0 && Math.abs(pinView.getWidth() - ssw('#ssw-community-box').width()) > 10) {
            pinView.resize();
          } else if (ssw('#ssw-user-feed-container:visible').length > 0 && Math.abs(userPinView.getWidth() - ssw('#ssw-community-box').width()) > 10) {
            userPinView.resize();
          }
        }
      };
      ssw(window).resize(redraw_cb);
      ssw(window).bind("orientationchange", redraw_cb);
      ssw('.ssw-community-body').scroll(function(){
        if (ssw('#ssw-faves-list:visible').length > 0 && ssw('#ssw-community-profile').actual('height') / 2 - 500 < ssw('.ssw-community-body').scrollTop()) {
          $favePins.showMore();
        }
        else if (ssw('#ssw-user-feed-container:visible').length > 0 && ssw('#ssw-community-profile').actual('height')/2 - 500 < ssw(this).scrollTop() && userPinView.getScrollChecker() == 0) {
          userPinView.onScroll();
        }
        else if (ssw('#ssw-feed-content:visible').length > 0 && ssw('#column-1').actual('height')/2 <  ssw(this).scrollTop() && pinView.getScrollChecker() == 0) {
          pinView.onScroll();
        }
        else if(ssw('#ssw-community-profile:visible').length){
          sswCmProfileScrollPosition = ssw(this).scrollTop();
          if(ssw(this).scrollTop() > 30){
            ssw('.ssw-link-feed').css('opacity', 0.5);
          }else{
            ssw('.ssw-link-feed').css('opacity', 1);
          }
        }
        else if(ssw('#ssw-community-action:visible').length){
          if(ssw(this).scrollTop() > 30){
            ssw('.ssw-link-back').css('opacity', 0.5);
          }else{
            ssw('.ssw-link-back').css('opacity', 1);
          }
        }
        else if(ssw('#ssw-feed-content:visible').length){
          sswCmFeedScrollPosition = ssw(this).scrollTop();
        }
      });

      setInterval(function() {
        if (communityActive) {
          pinView.newItemChecker();
        }
      }, 20000);
      ssw('#ssw-community-feed #ssw-feed-updates-count').click(function(){
        pinView.uploadNewItems();
      });
      ssw('#ssw-community-feed .tab-switcher').click(function(){
        ssw('#feed-is-empty').hide();
        ssw('#feed-switch-loader').show();
        pinView.setType(ssw(this).attr('id'));
        pinView.reloader();
        ssw(this).parent().find('.ssw-tab').removeClass('ssw-active');
        ssw(this).addClass('ssw-active');
      });
    });
  });
  //______________________report_____________________\\
  function sendReport(item_id) {
    ssw('#ssw-feed-content #feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-options-body').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-feed-content #feed-item-' + item_id + ' .cmreportbox').show();
  }
  function deletePinConfirm(item_id) {
    ssw('#ssw-feed-content #feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-options-body').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-feed-content #feed-item-' + item_id + ' .deletebox').show();
    ssw('#ssw-user-feed-container #user-feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-options-body').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-user-feed-container #user-feed-item-' + item_id + ' .deletebox').show();
  }
  function deleteCommConfirm(comment_id, item_id) {
    var element = ssw(ssw('.comment-' + comment_id)[0]);
    element.children().hide();
    element.find('.ssw-comment-delete-btn').css('opacity', 0);
    var commentConfirmDiv = ssw('<div>', {class:"ssw-comment-confirm"}).append(
      ssw('<a>', {class:'ssw-comment-delete btn', 'data-id': comment_id}).text('Delete')
    ).append(ssw('<a>', {class:'comment-cancel', 'data-id': comment_id}).text('Cancel'));
    element.append(commentConfirmDiv);
  }

  ssw(document).on('click', '#ssw-feed-content .delete-item', function(){
    var item_id = parseInt(ssw(this).attr('data-item-id'));
    ssw.ajax({
      url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/feed/index/deletepin',
      type: 'POST',
      data: {'_sid':ssw.cookie('hesid'), 'item-id': item_id},
      beforeSend: function () {
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-report-loader').show();
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .deletebox').hide();
        ssw(this).removeAttr('enabled').attr('disabled', 'disabled');
      },
      dataType: 'json',
      success: function () {
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id).fadeOut(1000, function(){
          ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id).remove();
          pinView.removeItem(item_id);
          pinView.resize();
          if(ssw('.ssw-feed-item').length == 0) {
            ssw('#ssw-feed-content').html('<div id="feed-is-empty" style="text-align: center; display: block;">' + sswLangs.t('feed_is_empty') + '</div>');
          }
        });
      }
    });
  });

  ssw(document).on('click', '#ssw-feed-content .send-report', function(){
    var item_id = parseInt(ssw(this).attr('data-item-id'));
    ssw.ajax({
      url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/feed/index/report',
      type: 'POST',
      data: {'action_id': item_id, '_sid': ssw.cookie("hesid")},
      beforeSend: function () {
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-report-loader').show();
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .cmreportbox').hide();
        ssw(this).removeAttr('enabled').attr('disabled', 'disabled');
      },
      dataType: 'json',
      success: function () {
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .report-btn').remove();
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' rep').remove();
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-report-loader').hide();
        ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-options-body').show();
      }
    });
  });

  ssw(document).on('click', '#ssw-feed-content .comment-cancel', function(){
    var comment_id = ssw(this).attr('data-id');
    var element = ssw(ssw('.comment-' + comment_id)[0]);
    element.find('div.ssw-comment-confirm').remove();
    element.children().show();
    element.find('.ssw-comment-delete-btn').css('opacity', '');
    element.find('.ssw-comment-delete-btn').css('display', 'block');
  });

  ssw(document).on('click', '#ssw-feed-content .ssw-comment-delete', function(){
    ssw(this).attr('disabled', 'disabled');
    var comment_id = ssw(this).attr('data-id');
    var element = ssw(ssw('.comment-' + comment_id)[0]);
    ssw.ajax({
      url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/core/comments/deletecomment',
      type: 'POST',
      data: {'_sid':ssw.cookie('hesid'), 'comment-id': comment_id},
      beforeSend: function () {
        element.find('div.ssw-comment-confirm').remove();
        var loader = ssw('<div>', {class: 'ssw-report-loader', style:'display:block;height:48px;'});
        element.append(loader);
      },
      dataType: 'json',
      success: function () {
        element.fadeOut(2000, function(){
          element.remove();
        });
      }
    });
  });

  ssw(document).on('click', '#ssw-feed-content .cancel-report', function(){
    var item_id = parseInt(ssw(this).attr('data-item-id'));
    ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .cmreportbox').hide();
    ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .deletebox').hide();
    ssw('#feed-item-' + item_id + ' .ssw-options-body').show();
  });

  ssw('#ssw-community-close').click(function(){
    ssw('.ssw-community-backdrop').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-community-box').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-community-icon').animate({bottom:'0'}, 500, function(){});
    communityActive = 0;
    if (ssw('#ssw-community-box').css('position') == 'absolute') {
      ssw(window).scrollTop(sswCmWindowScrollPosition);
    }
  });

  ssw('#ssw-community-icon').click(function(){
    sswCmWindowScrollPosition = ssw(window).scrollTop();
    if (ssw('#ssw-community-box').css('position') == 'absolute') {
      ssw(window).scrollTop(0);
    }
    ssw('#ssw-community-box').fadeIn();
    ssw('#ssw-community-icon').css('bottom','-100px');
    communityActive = 1;
    trackShopStats('feed_view', 'all');
    if(ssw('.ssw-community-backdrop').length){
      ssw('.ssw-community-backdrop').show();
    }else{
      ssw('<div>', { class: 'ssw-community-backdrop' }).appendTo(document.body)
    }
  });

  ssw('#ssw-link-feed, .ssw-link-feed').click(function(){
    ssw('#ssw-community-action').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-community-profile').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-community-feed').show();
    ssw('.ssw-community-body').scrollTop(sswCmFeedScrollPosition);
    ssw('#ssw-community-action > .ssw-action-body').empty();
  });

  ssw('#ssw-link-profile').click(function(){
    ssw('#ssw-community-action').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-community-feed').hide();
    ssw('#ssw-community-profile').show();
    ssw('.ssw-community-body').scrollTop(sswCmProfileScrollPosition);
    ssw('#ssw-community-action > .ssw-action-body').empty();
  });

  ssw(document.body).on('click', '#ssw-community-box a[href*="/pages/profile/"]', function(){
    if(ssw('#ssw-community-box:hidden').length){
      ssw('#ssw-community-icon').click();
    }
    sswCmProfileScrollPosition = 0;
    ssw('#ssw-community-profile > .ssw-profile-body').html('<div class="ssw-page-loading"><span class="ssw-spin ssw-loader"></span></div>');
    if(ssw('#ssw-community-profile:hidden').length){
      ssw('#ssw-community-feed').hide();
      ssw('#ssw-community-action').hide();
      ssw('#ssw-community-profile').fadeIn();
    }

    var userHrefData = ssw(this).attr('href').split('/');
    ssw.get(sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/profile/' + userHrefData[userHrefData.length-1] + '/', { 
'_sid': sswCookie('hesid'), 
'hash_key': sswCookie('hash_key'), 
'feed_type': 'community' 
}, function(response){
      ssw('#ssw-community-profile > .ssw-profile-body').css('opacity', 0);
      ssw('#ssw-community-profile > .ssw-profile-body').html(response).animate({
        opacity: "1"
      }, 300);
    });
    return false;
  });

  sswSettings = {
    'shop_amount': "${{amount}}",
    'cart_count_id': '#cart-count',
    'cart_total_id': '#cart-total',
    'go_to_cart': 0,
    'fave_enabled': 1,
    'fave_with_variant': 1,
    'fave_multiple_variant': 0,
    'review_enabled': 0,
    'askfriends_enabled': 0,
    'comments_enabled': 1  };

  ssw(document.body).on('click', '.ssw-community-backdrop', function(){
    ssw('#ssw-community-close').click();
  });
  sswLazyLoad(document.querySelector('#ssw-community-box'), '.ssw-item-image img');
  setTimeout(function () {
    ssw('#ssw-community-icon.logotype-url > span').css('left', '80px');
  }, 1500);
</script>

  function sswRewardLibraryLoaded() {
    var sswRewardPopupSettings = {};
    sswRewardPopupSettings = {"tab_title":"Rewards","tab_position":"right","tab_logo":"","tab_rounded":"1","popup_exit_intent":"0","popup_auto":"0","popup_after":"15"};
    sswRewardPopupSettings.viewer_points = undefined;
    new sswRewardPopup(ssw, sswRewardPopupSettings);
  }
  if (!window.sswLibraryJsLoaded) {
    document.addEventListener('sswlibraryload', function () {
      sswRewardLibraryLoaded();
    });
  } else {
    sswRewardLibraryLoaded();
  }
  ssw('.ssw-reward-box-rule-description').click(function () {
        ssw(`div[data-rule-name="${ssw(this).data('earning-rule')}"]`).fadeToggle()
  })
</script>
